Extensive evaluations on skin lesion (Derm7pt), ocular disease (ODIR), and meningioma (MEN) datasets demonstrate that CMML significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, yielding AVG AUC improvements of 1.26%, 0.97%, and 1.32%, respectively.

</details>


### [180] [PathWISE: Multi-Agent Cancer Pathway Triaging Ontology Learning from Clinical Flowcharts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25970)
*Sofiat Abioye,Ufaq Khan,Shazad Ashraf,Mohammed Adil Butt,Andrew D. Beggs,Adam Byfield,Anusha Jose,William Poulett,Ben Wallace,Junaid Qadir,Muhammad Bilal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathWISE是一个五阶段流水线，结合四个LLM智能体、确定性深度优先搜索审计器和Java编译器，将临床路径流程图转化为可执行的HL7 CQL库，在五个NHS癌症路径上实现100%编译成功和零幻觉术语代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床路径以可视化流程图形式传播，其空间拓扑、箭头方向、颜色编码和字体粗细编码了关键分诊逻辑，但这些信息对计算系统不可访问，亟需将非可计算的人工制品转化为可执行的计算化临床决策支持服务。

Method: 五阶段流水线：1) LLM智能体提取流程图结构为类型化有向图；2) 确定性路径枚举；3) 结构化语义审计每个节点的可计算性；4) 生成经官方Java CQL-to-ELM编译器验证的术语约束CQL定义；5) 产生覆盖100%枚举患者路径的路由逻辑。非确定性LLM推理限定于知识提取，确定性图数学和标准编译器支撑所有验证步骤。

Result: 在五个UK NHS癌症路径上测试，审计最多183个节点（Hybrid配置下182个），识别544个结构化治理发现（四个问题类别），达到100%语法编译成功，不可计算节点使用假占位符保持可编译性同时暴露治理缺口，为零幻觉术语代码。

Conclusion: PathWISE成功地将非计算化的临床流程图转化为可验证、可执行的CQL库，通过将LLM的非确定性限制在知识提取阶段，而用确定性方法保证验证的可靠性，为临床路径的数字化落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Clinical pathways are disseminated as visual flowcharts where spatial topology, arrow direction, colour coding, and font weight encode critical triage logic that remains inaccessible to computational systems. We present PathWISE, a five-phase pipeline combining four LLM-based agents with a deterministic depth-first search auditor and a Java compiler critic, transforming these non-computable artefacts into validated, executable HL7 Clinical Quality Language (CQL) libraries deployable as FHIR CDS Hooks services. Purpose-built agents extract flowchart structure into a typed directed graph, perform deterministic path enumeration, conduct a structured semantic audit of every node's computability, generate terminology-constrained CQL definitions verified by the official Java CQL-to-ELM compiler, and produce routing logic covering 100% of enumerated patient journeys. Demonstrated across five UK NHS cancer pathways (colorectal, lung, skin, upper GI, and breast), PathWISE audits up to 183 nodes (182 under the Hybrid configuration), identifies 544 structured governance findings across four issue categories, achieves 100% syntactic compilation success, with UNCOMPUTABLE nodes receiving false placeholders that preserve compilability while surfacing governance gaps for clinical review, and produces zero hallucinated terminology codes for dictionary-covered concepts. Critically, PathWISE confines non-deterministic LLM inference to knowledge extraction while deterministic graph mathematics and a standard compiler underpin every verification step.

</details>


### [181] [LLaVA-OneVision-2: Towards Next-Generation Perceptual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25979)
*Xiang An,Yin Xie,Feilong Tang,Yunyao Yan,Huajie Tan,Didi Zhu,Changrui Chen,Xiuwei Zhao,Bin Qin,Kaicheng Yang,Yifei Shen,Yuanhan Zhang,Kaichen Zhang,Wenkang Zhang,Zheng Cheng,Nansen Zhang,Chunsheng Wu,Chunjiang Ge,Zimin Ran,Dehua Song,Chunyuan Li,Shikun Feng,Ming Hu,Zhangquan Chen,Junbo Niu,Bo Li,Ziyong Feng,Ziwei Liu,Zongyuan Ge,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LLaVA-OneVision-2 (LLaVA-OV-2) 提出了一种基于编解码器流令牌化的视觉-语言模型，通过码流动态与运动残差线索实现自适应视频令牌压缩，并引入JumpScore基准测试，在视频理解、时空定位与跟踪任务上大幅超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在处理长视频时面临令牌预算有限的问题，基于固定图像组的压缩方式无法有效聚焦事件承载内容，且缺乏针对高频率、密集重复运动的细粒度时间定位评估基准。

Method: 基于原生OneVision-Encoder与窗口注意力机制；提出编解码器流令牌化，利用码流比特成本动态决定自适应时间分组，运动残差线索选择空间显著区域并压缩至紧凑视觉画布；采用共享3D旋转位置编码统一编解码器画布、采样帧和图像的空间-时间坐标。

Result: 在JumpScore上，8B模型达74.9 mAP（比Qwen3-VL-8B提升44.8点）；同令牌预算下编解码器流对比帧采样提升9.7点；视频任务平均提升4.3点，空间任务提升5.3点，跟踪任务J&F提升15.6点。

Conclusion: LLaVA-OV-2通过码流令牌化实现了高效的长视频令牌压缩与统一的多模态感知，验证了开放监督大规模训练的有效性，为视频理解、时空定位与跟踪提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We introduce LLaVA-OneVision-2 (LLaVA-OV-2), the most capable vision-language model in the LLaVA-OneVision series to date, achieving superior performance across a broad range of multimodal benchmarks. The model builds on a native OneVision-Encoder and incorporates Windowed Attention for efficient local computation while maintaining native resolution. Its key advance is codec-stream tokenization: it treats compressed video as a continuous bit-cost stream, where bit-cost dynamics determine adaptive temporal groups, and motion-residual cues select salient spatial evidence into compact visual canvases. This allocation concentrates a limited token budget on event-bearing content, enabling more stable long-video token compression than fixed groups of pictures. A shared 3D RoPE further places codec canvases, sampled frames, and images in a unified spatiotemporal coordinate system. Furthermore, we build the LLaVA-OV-2 data and training stack around large-scale open supervision: approximately 8M re-captioned video samples for pretraining, a 4M-sample spatial corpus for fine-tuning. We also introduce JumpScore, a temporal-localization benchmark targeting fine-grained grounding in high-frequency, densely repeated motion, a regime underrepresented by existing video evaluations. A standout capability of LLaVA-OV-2 is its unified perception across video understanding, temporal grounding, spatial grounding, and manipulation-trace reasoning. On JumpScore, LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B reaches 74.9 JumpScore mAP, surpassing Qwen3-VL-8B (30.1) by +44.8 points; under matched visual-token budgets on the same benchmark, codec-stream inputs improve temporal grounding over frame sampling by +9.7 points. Across standard benchmarks, LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B further outperforms Qwen3-VL-8B by +4.3 average points on video tasks, +5.3 on spatial tasks, and +15.6 average J&F on tracking tasks.

</details>


### [182] [MIND: Multi-Scale Intent Diffusion for Text-Driven Physics-Based Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26006)
*Bin Li,Ruichi Zhang,Han Liang,Jingyan Zhang,Juze Zhang,Xin Chen,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIND框架，利用行为意图作为语义桥梁，通过多尺度意图扩散机制实现从文本命令到物理人形机器人控制的端到端生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么受限于运动学生成与物理跟踪之间的域偏移，要么难以弥合文本命令与低层动作之间的模态鸿沟，缺乏有效的语义对齐。

Method: 提出MIND框架，核心是多尺度意图扩散机制：整体意图预测器捕获全局行为动态，即时意图预测器提供逐步骤的细粒度信号；同时将人形状态编码到潜在空间以增强语义意图建模。

Result: MIND在语义对齐、行为自然性和物理合理性方面均优于现有方法，能够从文本命令合成连贯且物理合理的动作。

Conclusion: 利用人形状态作为语义桥梁，通过分层次的行为意图归纳偏置，有效弥合了文本与物理控制之间的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Enabling physics-based humanoids to execute diverse behaviors from high-level textual commands remains a significant challenge. Existing methods typically follow either a two-stage paradigm that combines kinematic motion generation with physics-based tracking, or an end-to-end imitation-learning paradigm that directly generates actions from text. However, the former suffers from the inherent domain shift between kinematic generation and physics-based tracking, while the latter struggles with the substantial modality gap between textual commands and low-level actions, limiting effective semantic alignment. Notably, humanoid states encode rich motion dynamics that are more semantically aligned with textual descriptions than low-level actions, making them a natural basis for deriving behavioral intent. Building upon this insight, we propose MIND, a novel end-to-end diffusion framework for text-driven physics-based humanoid control that leverages behavioral intent as a semantic bridge between textual commands and low-level actions. At its core, MIND introduces a multi-scale intent diffusion mechanism, where a holistic intent predictor captures global behavioral dynamics to guide overall behavior synthesis, while an immediate intent predictor provides step-wise, fine-grained signals for local behavior refinement at each diffusion step. This hierarchical intent formulation imposes a structured inductive bias for humanoid control, improving semantic alignment and behavioral naturalness. Furthermore, MIND encodes humanoid states into a latent space to enable more effective semantic intent modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MIND outperforms existing methods and synthesizes coherent, physically plausible, and semantically aligned humanoid behaviors from text commands. Our code will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [183] [Everything at Every Scale: Scale-Invariant Diffusion with Continuous Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26032)
*Zixin Jessie Chen,Zhuo Chen,Archer Wang,Jeff Gore,William T. Freeman,Congyue Deng,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SKILD 是一种尺度不变的 K 空间图像学习扩散模型，通过将尺度作为扩散过程的显式坐标，统一了图像生成和连续超分辨率任务。同一无条件模型可通过改变起始时间步执行两种任务，无需任务特定架构、条件分支或重训练，在 CIFAR-10 无条件生成上达到 FID 2.65，并在 ImageNet 超分辨率上超越条件模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成和超分辨率本质上都可理解为逆转不同尺度上的信息丢失，但现有方法通常将它们视为独立任务。作者利用自然图像和物理系统共有的尺度不变性，设计统一框架处理两者。

Method: 设计了一个前向过程，从细尺度到粗尺度衰减图像内容，同时注入与频谱匹配的高斯噪声，将尺度作为扩散动力学的显式坐标。训练后，同一逆向模型通过改变起始时间步即可执行生成或连续超分辨率，无需任何任务特定修改。

Result: CIFAR-10 无条件生成 FID 2.65、Inception Score 9.63；ImageNet 上 2×–8× 超分辨率超越条件模型；在临界 Ising 模型中重建的四点关联函数与真实值高度吻合。

Conclusion: SKILD 证明了尺度不变性可以有效地统一生成与超分辨率任务，提供了一种简洁而强大的框架，无需任务专用架构或条件机制即可在多个任务上取得优异性能。

Abstract: Creating images from noise is image generation; reconstructing fine details from coarse inputs is super-resolution. Despite their practical differences, both can be understood as reversing information loss across scales. We introduce $\textbf{SKILD}$, a $\textbf{S}$cale-invariant $\textbf{K}$-Space $\textbf{I}$mage $\textbf{L}$earning $\textbf{D}$iffusion model that unifies generation and continuous super-resolution within a single unconditional framework. Both natural images and critical physical systems exhibit scale invariance, and we leverage it to design a forward process that attenuates image content from fine to coarse scales while injecting spectrum-matched Gaussian noise, making scale an explicit coordinate of the diffusion dynamics. The same trained reverse process performs generation and continuous super-resolution by varying only the starting timestep: $\textit{no task-specific architecture, no conditioning branch, no classifier-free guidance, no retraining per scale factor}$. Empirically, SKILD reaches FID $2.65$ and Inception Score $9.63$ on unconditional CIFAR-10, performs $2\times$--$8\times$ super-resolution on ImageNet from a single unconditional checkpoint while outperforming conditional models across perceptual metrics, and reconstructs critical Ising models whose connected four-point correlations closely track the ground truth.

</details>


### [184] [DRScaffold: Boosting Dense-Scene Reasoning in Lightweight Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26038)
*Xinrui Shi,Kai Liu,Ziqing Zhang,Jianze Li,Anqi Li,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现轻量级视觉语言模型在密集场景推理中系统性地失败，为此构建了DRBench基准（14,573个问题，2,943张图像，分5个任务类别和3个推理层次），并提出DRScaffold监督微调框架，通过将监督目标分解为四个因果有序阶段来强制进行有依据的推理。实验表明，该方法在三个轻量级VLM上显著提升了密集场景推理能力，且Qwen2.5-VL-3B经DRScaffold训练后超越了冻结参数的Qwen2.5-VL-32B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级VLM在标准基准上表现良好，但在需要多个对象、属性和关系联合定位并通过多步推理解决的密集场景推理中系统性失败，因为现有训练信号无法在推理步骤与底层视觉实体/关系之间建立明确依据，导致模型生成流畅但缺乏视觉锚定的推理链条。

Method: 1）构建DRBench基准，含14,573个问题、2,943张图像，覆盖5个任务类别和3个渐进推理层次；2）提出DRScaffold监督微调框架，将监督目标分解为四个因果有序阶段，在不修改架构的情况下强制进行有依据的推理。

Result: 在三个轻量级VLM上，DRScaffold在DRBench上取得了显著提升，同时保持或提升了通用基准的性能。Qwen2.5-VL-3B经DRScaffold训练后，在DRBench上超越了冻结参数的Qwen2.5-VL-32B。

Conclusion: 结构化监督可以在密集场景推理中替代相当一部分模型规模带来的能力提升，为轻量级模型在复杂视觉场景中的可靠推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Lightweight vision-language models perform competitively on standard benchmarks yet fail systematically in dense-scene reasoning, where multiple objects, attributes, and relations must be jointly grounded and resolved through multi-step inference. Such capability is critical for real-world applications where models must reliably interpret cluttered environments. Yet existing training signals provide no explicit grounding between reasoning steps and the underlying visual entities and relations, leaving lightweight models free to generate fluent but visually unanchored reasoning chains. To address this gap, we first introduce DRBench, a benchmark of 14,573 questions across 2,943 images, organized into five task categories spanning three progressive reasoning layers. Building on DRBench, we propose DRScaffold, a supervised fine-tuning framework that decomposes the supervision target into four causally ordered stages, enforcing grounded reasoning without architectural modification. Experiments on three lightweight VLMs demonstrate substantial gains on DRBench while preserving or improving performance on general-purpose benchmarks. Notably, Qwen2.5-VL-3B trained with DRScaffold surpasses the frozen Qwen2.5-VL-32B on DRBench, demonstrating that structured supervision can substitute for a significant portion of model scale in dense-scene reasoning. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/irene-shi/DRScaffold .

</details>


### [185] [Paris 2.0: A Decentralized Diffusion Model for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26064)
*Ali Rouzbayani,Bidhan Roy,Marcos Villagra,Zhiying Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paris 2.0是首个通过去中心化计算预训练的视频生成模型，在低分辨率文本到视频训练中，相比单体模型将FVD从561.04降至279.01（约2倍提升），并提高了CLIP文本-视频相似度和美学评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化训练下，时间一致的视频生成一直是一个未解决的开放问题，Paris 2.0旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于Paris 1.0（去中心化扩散模型DDM）的训练方案，通过去中心化计算进行预训练，扩展图像生成到视频生成领域。

Result: 在低分辨率文本到视频训练中，相比同等计算预算下在相同数据上训练的单体模型，FVD从561.04降至279.01（约2.0倍提升），CLIP文本-视频相似度和美学评分均有所提高。

Conclusion: Paris 2.0成功证明了去中心化训练可用于时间一致的视频生成，且性能优于同等计算条件下的单体模型。

Abstract: We present Paris 2.0, the first video generation model pre-trained through decentralized computation. Its training recipe builds upon Paris 1.0 (arXiv:2510.03434), the first ever open-weight Decentralized Diffusion Model (DDM), which showed that image generation can be trained without a monolithic GPU cluster. However, temporally coherent video generation had remained an open problem under decentralized training, and Paris 2.0 closes it.
  In low-resolution text-to-video training, against a monolithic model trained on the same data under a matched total compute budget, Paris 2.0 cuts Frechet Video Distance (FVD) from 561.04 to 279.01, a ~2.0x improvement, and lifts CLIP text-video similarity and aesthetic score.

</details>


### [186] [Channel-wise Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26089)
*Wei Song,Tianhang Wang,Yitong Chen,Tong Zhang,Zuxuan Wu,Ming Li,Jiaqi Wang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通道级向量量化（CVQ）和通道级自回归（CAR）模型，将图像表示为离散的视觉细节层次而非空间网格，通过"下一通道预测"机制逐步生成从全局结构到细节纹理的图像，实现100%码本利用率和优异的文本到图像生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的向量量化（VQ）将每个图像块的特征向量映射为离散token，将图像视为空间网格的排列，这种"逐块预测"的方式忽略了图像中视觉细节的层次化结构，且存在码本利用率低的问题。作者希望提出一种新的图像分词范式，能够更自然地捕捉从全局结构到局部细节的图像生成过程。

Method: (1) 通道级向量量化（CVQ）：对特征图的每个通道而非每个空间块进行量化，将图像表示为离散的视觉细节层次（channels as levels of visual details）；(2) 通道级自回归（CAR）模型：采用"下一通道预测"策略，按通道顺序自回归生成图像，先预测全局结构通道，再逐步预测精细纹理通道，模拟人类艺术家的创作流程。

Result: (1) CVQ在16K+码本大小下实现100%码本利用率，无需任何额外技巧，重建质量显著优于传统VQ；(2) CAR模型在文本到图像生成任务上达到DPG得分86.7、GenEval得分0.79。

Conclusion: 通道级向量量化作为一种全新的图像表示范式，有效提升了码本利用率和重建质量；基于此的通道级自回归模型通过从全局到细节的渐进式生成策略，展示了在文本到图像生成中的强大潜力。

Abstract: We present Channel-wise Vector Quantization (CVQ), a novel image tokenization paradigm that replaces patch-wise tokens with channel-wise tokens. Unlike conventional vector quantization, which assigns a discrete token to each patch feature vector, CVQ quantizes each channel of the feature map. This formulation represents an image as discrete levels of visual details, rather than as a grid of spatial patches. Based on CVQ, we introduce a new visual autoregressive framework with "next-channel prediction". Instead of rendering images patch by patch in raster order, our Channel-wise Autoregressive (CAR) model predicts image channels sequentially, producing progressively enriched visual details. Specifically, it first sketches global structure and then refines fine-grained attributes, akin to a human artist's workflow. Empirically, we show that: (1) CVQ achieves 100% codebook utilization with a 16K+ codebook size without any bells and whistles, and substantially improves reconstruction quality over conventional VQ; and (2) CAR attains a DPG score of 86.7 and a GenEval score of 0.79, demonstrating strong effectiveness for text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [187] [InstructSAM: Segment Any Instance with Any Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26102)
*Yuqian Yuan,Wentong Li,Zhaocheng Li,Yutong Lin,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InstructSAM统一框架，通过将可学习实例查询注入VLM并结合混合注意力机制，实现基于任意指令的多实例分割，仅需单次前向传播即可完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在复杂指令理解和多实例分割时，通常需要多步流水线或修改模型架构，缺乏统一高效的端到端解决方案。

Method: 1) 将指令驱动实例分割建模为集合结构查询预测问题；2) 设计推理到实例查询接口，在VLM中注入可学习实例查询并与指令和视觉信息上下文化；3) 混合注意力机制促进查询、视觉token和指令token交互；4) 将LLM条件化查询投影到SAM3检测器查询空间。

Result: 仅2B参数规模下，在复杂指令驱动和短语级指代分割基准上取得强结果，超越先前端到端方法和SAM3的智能体流水线，实现高效单次多实例预测。

Conclusion: InstructSAM无需修改SAM3核心架构，即可赋予其高层指令理解、组合推理和实例级集合预测能力，并构建了高质量大规模指令实例分割数据集Inst2Seg用于训练评估。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce InstructSAM, a unified and streamlined framework designed for multi-instance segmentation under arbitrary instructions. We formulates instruction-driven instance segmentation as a set-structured query prediction problem and propose an explicit reasoning-to-instance query interface that elegantly bridges a vision-language model (VLM) and SAM3. Specifically, a bank of learnable instance queries is injected into the VLM and contextualized with instruction and visual information, enabling each query to serve as an instance-aware slot. A hybrid-attention mechanism further promotes interaction among these queries, visual tokens, and instruction tokens, improving instance enumeration and reducing duplicate predictions. The resulting LLM-conditioned queries are projected into SAM3's detector query space to drive accurate multi-instance segmentation in a single forward pass. This design equips SAM3 with high-level instruction understanding, compositional reasoning, and instance-level set prediction without modifying its core architecture. To support training and evaluation, we further construct Inst2Seg, a high-quality and large-scale instruction-based instance segmentation dataset and benchmark that couples free-form instructions with instance-level masks. Extensive experiments show that only 2B-scale InstructSAM achieves strong results across complex instruction-driven and phrase-level referring segmentation benchmarks, outperforming prior end-to-end methods and SAM3's agentic pipeline while enabling efficient single-pass multi-instance prediction.

</details>


### [188] [Global Structure-from-Motion Meets Feedforward Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26103)
*Linfei Pan,Johannes Schönberge,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合经典SfM方法和前馈3D重建技术优势的新型Structure-from-Motion流水线，在多种场景下实现了最先进的重建效果，并开源了实现代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SfM方法在低纹理、低重叠和对称性等场景下存在鲁棒性问题，而前馈3D重建方法虽在这些挑战场景中表现出色，但在标准场景中的可扩展性、精度和鲁棒性方面不如经典方法。作者旨在结合两者优势，克服各自的局限性。

Method: 系统分析经典SfM和前馈3D重建方法的局限性，设计了一种新型SfM流水线，将经典方法的可扩展性和精度与前馈方法在困难场景下的鲁棒性相结合。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在广泛的场景下取得了最先进的重建结果。

Conclusion: 通过融合经典和前馈方法的各自优势，能够构建出在多种场景下均表现优异的SfM系统，为后续研究提供了开源实现。

Abstract: Structure-from-Motion -- the process of simultaneously estimating camera poses and 3D scene structure from a collection of images -- remains a central challenge in computer vision, with many open problems yet to be solved. Recent advances in feedforward 3D reconstruction have made significant strides in overcoming persistent failure cases of classical SfM methods, particularly in scenarios characterized by low texture, limited overlap, and symmetries. However, while feedforward approaches excel in these challenging conditions, they often face limitations regarding scalability, accuracy, or robustness, and typically fall short of classical methods in standard reconstruction settings. In this work, we systematically analyze these limitations and propose a new Structure-from-Motion pipeline by combining the respective strengths of classical and feedforward methods. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets show the benefits of our approach, achieving state-of-the-art results across a wide range of scenarios. We share our system as an open-source implementation at https://github.com/colmap/gluemap.

</details>


### [189] [EVIDENT: Routing MLLM Adaptation through Entity-Grounded Visual Evidence for Cross-Domain Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26104)
*Geo Ahn,Jiwook Han,Youngrae Kim,Joonseok Lee,Jinwoo Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EVIDENT框架，通过实体瓶颈适配器、实体绑定蒸馏损失和实体到证据门控机制，将视频时序定位微调锚定在预训练MLLM的固有实体注意力上，有效缓解域偏移下的性能退化问题，在跨域基准上提升了鲁棒性并保持了域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在VTG任务上微调后，域内性能提升但域迁移下性能急剧下降。作者发现该失效不仅由未见查询概念导致，更关键的是视觉域偏移阻碍了模型将时序定位知识与固有实体注意力能力耦合。

Method: 提出EVIDENT框架，包含三个组件：(i) 实体瓶颈适配器(Entity Bottleneck Adapter)将密集视觉token压缩为紧凑的实体级槽位；(ii) 实体绑定蒸馏损失(Entity-Binding Distillation)将物体性先验注入语义非结构化的MLLM视觉空间，引导每个槽位绑定到连贯实体；(iii) 实体到证据门控机制(Entity-to-eVidence gating)利用捕获的实体作为证据，引导模型定位包含查询相关实体的时刻。

Result: 在跨域VTG基准上，EVIDENT持续提升了域外鲁棒性，同时以适中的参数开销保持了具有竞争力的域内性能。

Conclusion: 实体级语义锚定是一种有效的归纳偏置，能够提升时序定位的可泛化性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning MLLMs for Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) often improves in-domain performance but degrades sharply under domain shift. In this work, we find that this failure is primarily driven not just by unseen query concepts, but by visual domain shift, which prevents the model from coupling its learned temporal localization knowledge with its inherent entity-attention capability. To address this, we introduce EVIDENT, a parameter-efficient adaptation framework that anchors temporal grounding in the inherent entity-attention of pre-trained MLLMs by routing VTG adaptation through explicit visual entity evidence. EVIDENT consists of three components: (i) an Entity Bottleneck Adapter that transforms dense visual tokens into compact entity-level slots, (ii) an Entity-Binding Distillation loss that instills objectness priors into the semantically unstructured MLLM visual space, guiding each slot to bind to a coherent entity, and (iii) an Entity-to-eVidence gating mechanism that leverages the captured entities as evidence, steering the model to localize moments containing query-relevant entities. Together, these components enable VTG fine-tuning to rely on entity-grounded evidence rather than brittle dataset shortcuts. Experiments on cross-domain VTG benchmarks show that EVIDENT consistently improves out-of-domain robustness while preserving competitive in-domain performance with modest parameter overhead. These results suggest that entity-level grounding is an effective inductive bias for generalizable temporal localization.

</details>


### [190] [On-Policy Adversarial Flow Distillation for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26105)
*Yang Luo,Shengju Qian,Xiaohang Tang,Zirui Zhu,Yong Liu,Xin Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗流蒸馏（AFD），一种用于异构黑盒教师视频蒸馏的在线策略框架。AFD通过查询教师和学生生成相同提示的视频，训练Bradley-Terry判别器估计师生差异，并将在线策略优势转化为前向流匹配更新，无需教师分数、潜在表示、去噪轨迹或反向链强化学习。实验表明AFD能显著提升因果自回归学生模型的运动与物理感知生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成器在流式、长时域和交互式应用中具有吸引力，但从强黑盒教师蒸馏到因果学生模型面临困难：学生需在其自身rollout分布下学习，而教师仅暴露提示条件化的完整视频，且在架构、容量、时域设计和采样调度上可能不同。这种接口使得监督微调属于离策略、基于分数的蒸馏不适用、直接对抗模仿的去噪时间信用分配过于稀疏。

Method: AFD是一个在线策略框架：1）在相同提示上查询教师和rollout当前学生；2）训练提示配对的Bradley-Terry判别器来估计干净样本上的师生差异；3）将在线策略优势转化为前向流程上的流匹配更新，作用在学生自身的噪声状态上。该方法无需教师分数、潜在表示、去噪轨迹、步骤对齐或反向链强化学习。

Result: 在两个因果自回归学生家族上的实验表明，AFD持续提升了运动敏感和物理敏感的视频生成质量，同时保持了通用视频质量。消融实验验证了自适应在线策略反馈和前向流程信用分配的重要性。

Conclusion: AFD仅需干净的教师视频和学生rollout，为将专有或异构视频生成器蒸馏为高效自回归学生模型提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generators are attractive for streaming, long-horizon, and interactive applications, but distilling strong black-box teachers into causal students remains difficult. The student must learn under its own rollout distribution, whereas practical teachers may expose only prompt-conditioned completed videos and may differ in architecture, capacity, temporal design, and sampling schedule. This interface makes supervised fine-tuning off-policy, score-based distillation inapplicable, and direct adversarial imitation too sparse for denoising-time credit assignment. We propose Adversarial Flow Distillation (AFD), an on-policy framework for heterogeneous black-box video distillation. AFD queries the teacher and rolls out the current student on the same prompts, trains a prompt-paired Bradley-Terry discriminator to estimate clean-sample teacher-student discrepancy, and converts the resulting on-policy advantage into forward-process flow-matching updates on the student's own noised states. Thus, AFD provides dense velocity-field supervision while requiring no teacher scores, latents, denoising trajectories, step alignment, or reverse-chain reinforcement learning. Experiments across two causal AR student families show that AFD consistently improves motion- and physics-sensitive generation while preserving general video quality, and ablations validate the importance of adaptive on-policy feedback and forward-process credit assignment. The method requires only clean teacher videos and student rollouts, providing a practical route for distilling proprietary or heterogeneous video generators into efficient autoregressive students.

</details>


### [191] [Reinforcing Few-step Generators via Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26108)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Ruoyu Wang,Chi Zhang,Jun Zhang,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RTDMD两阶段框架，统一分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导强化学习，在仅4步推理下实现少步文生图新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散蒸馏虽实现了高效图像生成，但将这些模型与人类偏好对齐仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段AC-DMD进行子区间分布匹配并添加一致性正则化；第二阶段联合优化分布匹配项和奖励最大化项，其中奖励最大化项采用混合策略梯度（结合GRPO风格估计器与直接奖励反向传播）及SubGRPO降方差。

Result: 在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上，仅4步推理即在偏好、美学和组合性指标上超越此前少步文生图方法，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: RTDMD有效统一了分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导强化学习，在少步生成中实现了高质量与人类偏好对齐的统一。

Abstract: Recent advances in few-step diffusion distillation have enabled efficient image generation, yet aligning these models with human preferences remains challenging. We propose Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching Distillation (RTDMD), a two-stage framework that unifies distribution matching distillation with reward-guided reinforcement learning for few-step flow generators. We show that minimizing the KL divergence to a reward-tilted teacher distribution naturally decomposes into a distribution matching term and a reward maximization term. In the first stage, we introduce Ambient-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (AC-DMD), which performs subinterval-wise distribution matching and augments the fake score objective with a consistency regularizer to help the fake score model track the shifting generator distribution under limited updates. In the second stage, we jointly optimize both terms: for the reward maximization term, we derive a hybrid policy gradient that combines a GRPO-style estimator for the stochastic intermediate transitions with direct reward backpropagation through the deterministic final step, and further introduce step-subset GRPO (SubGRPO) to reduce variance. Experiments on SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.2 demonstrate that RTDMD establishes new state-of-the-art results across preference, aesthetic, and compositional metrics with only 4 inference steps, outperforming previous few-step text-to-image generation methods. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD.

</details>


### [192] [Helix4D: Complex 4D Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26109)
*Jiraphon Yenphraphai,Jianqi Chen,Jian Wang,Gordon Qian,Sergey Tulyakov,Rameen Abdal,Raymond A. Yeh,Peter Wonka,Chaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Helix4D是一种基于Trellis2的动态网格生成框架，通过滑动窗口跨帧注意力机制和4D时间编码，将图像到3D的Trellis2扩展到视频到4D生成，有效处理复杂拓扑变化、透明材料和薄结构等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频到4D方法在处理复杂拓扑变化、透明材料、薄结构和内表面时存在困难，亟需一种能够继承预训练模型质量同时实现时序一致性的4D生成框架。

Method: (a) 滑动窗口跨帧注意力机制，以第一帧为锚点，通过跨帧注意力继承Trellis2在稀有案例（如透明物体、内表面）上的预训练质量；(b) 4D时间编码，将冗余的低频空间RoPE波段重新用于时间维度，无需额外参数即可将3D位置编码扩展为4D。

Result: 在ActionBench和自建复杂动态数据集上的大量实验表明，Helix4D在高质量动态网格生成方面效果显著。

Conclusion: Helix4D通过巧妙的设计解决了视频到4D生成的关键挑战，继承了Trellis2的优异表达能力，为动态网格生成提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Current video-to-4D methods struggle with complex topology changes, transparent materials, thin structures, and inner surfaces. We present Helix4D, a dynamic mesh generation framework by inheriting the expressive representation of Trellis2, adapting it from image-to-3D to video-conditioned 4D generation. Our design arises from two key questions: (a) how to enable Trellis2's frame-local attention to share information across frames while preserving its pretrained quality on rare cases such as transparent objects and inner surfaces, and (b) how to inject temporal information into a purely 3D positional encoding without breaking pretrained capabilities. We address (a) with a sliding-window cross-frame attention and anchor on the first frame. The first frame is generated by the base Trellis2 model and injected into our model, letting it inherit Trellis2's quality in rare cases through cross-frame attention. We address (b) with a 4D temporal encoding that repurposes redundant low-frequency spatial RoPE bands for time, extending the encoding from 3D with no additional parameters. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of Helix4D for high-quality dynamic mesh generation on ActionBench and our own challenging complex dynamics set.

</details>


### [193] [Squeezing Capacity from Multimodal Large Language Models for Subject-driven Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26111)
*Shuhong Zheng,Aashish Kumar Misraa,Yu-Teng Li,Yu-Jhe Li,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)和VAE身份条件的主题驱动图像生成方法，通过双层级聚合模块和多阶段去噪策略，在保持主体身份的同时遵循文本指令生成新图像，有效缓解了复制粘贴问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法分别编码文本和参考图像，限制了跨模态推理能力并导致复制粘贴伪影；而连接多模态模型与扩散模型的方法虽然改进了指令遵循，却忽略了身份保持。本文旨在同时实现多模态理解与身份保持。

Method: 1）将扩散模型条件设置为联合编码文本和参考图像的MLLM；2）增加基于VAE的身份条件；3）设计双层级聚合(DLA)模块聚合多层级MLLM特征；4）采用多阶段去噪策略，在推理过程中逐步平衡MLLM的语义信息与VAE的细粒度身份信息。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多模态理解与身份保持之间取得良好平衡，缓解了复制粘贴问题，在主题驱动图像生成的人类偏好评估中达到优越性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明联合MLLM与VAE身份条件的方法能有效提升主题驱动图像生成质量，兼顾指令遵循与身份保持，且优于现有方法。

Abstract: Subject-driven image generation aims to synthesize new images that preserve the identity of the given subject while following textual instructions. Existing approaches often encode text and reference images separately. This limits cross-modal reasoning abilities and causes copy-paste artifacts. Recent frameworks that connect multimodal models and diffusion models improve instruction following, but largely overlook identity preservation. To address these limitations, we condition diffusion models on Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) that jointly encode text and reference images, and augment it with VAE-based identity conditioning. A novel Dual Layer Aggregation (DLA) module is designed to aggregate multi-level MLLM features for optimal conditioning, and a multi-stage denoising strategy is applied to progressively balance the semantic information from MLLM and fine-detail identity from VAE during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach harmonizes multimodal understanding with identity preservation, mitigates copy-paste issues, and achieves superior performance regarding human preference on subject-driven image generation. Our project website is available at https://zsh2000.github.io/squeeze-mllm-subject-gen/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Document Classification Pattern Recognition via Information Fusion: A Systematic Review of Multimodal and Multiview Representation Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23910)
*Marcin Michał Mirończuk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述139篇文献，提出了信息融合的统一框架，并首次通过元分析量化了多模态融合（准确率提升+5.28%）和多视图融合（准确率+4.67%、F1+3.08%、召回率提升）对文档分类的改进效果，同时揭示了该领域方法论严谨性不足的问题（仅11.8%-23.3%的研究使用统计检验）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息融合在文档分类中广泛应用，但缺乏统一框架、定量效果综合和实用指南，本文旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 系统综述139篇文献，提出形式化框架，进行定性分析识别关键趋势，并执行随机效应元分析（首次聚焦文档分类）量化性能提升。

Result: 多模态融合提升准确率+5.28%（p=0.0016），F1方向性正向但不显著；多视图融合提升准确率+4.67%、F1+3.08%、召回率（均p<0.05）。仅11.8%（多模态）和23.3%（多视图）的研究使用了统计检验。

Conclusion: 成功的信息融合不依赖于算法复杂度，而在于融合方法与任务上下文的战略对齐以及更严格的验证。

Abstract: Information fusion is used widely to improve document classification by the integration of multiple data sources (multimodal) or representations (multiview). However, the field lacks a unified framework, a quantitative synthesis of its effectiveness, and clear guidance for practitioners. This systematic review addresses these gaps by analysing 139 primary studies. It introduces a formal framework to structure the field, presents the results of a qualitative analysis to identify key trends, and performs a random-effects meta-analysis (to our knowledge, the first focused on document classification) to quantify performance gains. Our meta-analysis reveals that multimodal fusion improves accuracy (mean gain of +5.28 percentage points, $p=0.0016$) significantly -- the F1-score effect is directionally positive but statistically non-significant in our primary model. Multiview fusion provides consistent but modest gains for accuracy (+4.67\%), F1-score (+3.08\%), and recall (all $p<0.05$). Critically, our qualitative synthesis uncovers challenges in reproducibility in methodological rigour: only 11.8\% (multimodal) and 23.3\% (multiview) of the studies use statistical tests to validate their findings, which undermines the reliability of many of their results. This review's primary contributions are a unifying framework, the first quantitative evidence base, and data-driven guidelines. This review concludes that successful information fusion depends not on algorithmic complexity, but on the strategic alignment of the fusion method with the task context and a commitment to more rigorous validation.

</details>


### [195] [Raon-Speech Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23912)
*Beomsoo Kim,Changho Choi,Dohyun Kim,Dongki Lee,Ethan Ewer,Eunchong Kim,Gyeongman Kim,Haechan Kim,Hyeonghwan Kim,Inkyu Park,Jihun Yun,Jihwan Moon,Jiyun Kim,Joonghyun Bae,Junhyuck Kim,Minkyu Kim,Sehun Lee,Seungjun Chung,Sungwoo Cho,Dongmin Park,Dongwon Kim,Hara Kang,Jonghyun Lee,Keon Lee,Kangwook Lee,Jaewoong Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Raon-Speech（9B参数语音语言模型，在英语和韩语的语音理解、回答和生成任务上表现优异）及其全双工扩展Raon-SpeechChat，通过三阶段训练（语音模块对齐、端到端预训练+知识蒸馏、多任务偏好优化后训练）在42个基准上超越Qwen2.5-Omni等模型，并开源所有模型、训练/推理流程和交互演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练LLM有效转化为既能理解又能生成语音、同时保留强文本能力的SpeechLM，并进一步实现自然全双工对话。

Method: 三阶段训练：(1) 语音模块对齐；(2) 端到端SpeechLM预训练+知识蒸馏；(3) 多任务偏好优化后训练。全双工扩展通过因果编码器适应、全双工预训练、全双工微调（语音和角色控制）三阶段在119K小时时间对齐的对话数据上持续训练。

Result: 在42个英语和韩语基准上，Raon-Speech在语音任务上优于Qwen2.5-Omni、Fun-Audio-Chat等8个同类模型，同时保持强文本QA性能。Raon-SpeechChat在FDB v1.0的轮流说话和中断敏感行为上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 证明了大规模高质量数据与精心设计的训练流程可有效构建高性能SpeechLM和全双工对话系统，并开源所有资源以推动领域发展。

Abstract: We present Raon-Speech, a top-performing 9B-parameter speech language model (SpeechLM) for English and Korean speech understanding, answering, and generation, and Raon-SpeechChat, a high-performing full-duplex extension for natural real-time conversation. Raon-Speech successfully transforms a pre-trained LLM into a SpeechLM that both understands and generates speech while preserving strong text capabilities. It trains on 1.38M hours of highly curated English and Korean speech and text datasets with the following training stages: (1) speech modules alignment, (2) end-to-end SpeechLM pre-training with knowledge distillation, and (3) multi-task preference optimization-based post-training. Across 42 English and Korean speech and text benchmarks, Raon-Speech establishes the strongest overall profile on speech-centric tasks in our comparison against eight similarly sized recent audio foundation models, including Qwen2.5-Omni and Fun-Audio-Chat, while preserving strong text question answering performance. Building upon it, Raon-SpeechChat enables natural full-duplex conversation by continual training on 119K hours of time-aligned real and synthetic dialogue data. It proceeds through three complementary training stages: (1) causal encoder adaptation, (2) full-duplex pre-training, (3) full-duplex fine-tuning for voice and role-control. On multiple full-duplex benchmarks, Raon-SpeechChat shows its clearest strengths on the turn-taking and interruption-sensitive behaviors covered by FDB v1.0, and remains competitive across the broader full-duplex evaluation suite. We open-source all model checkpoints, the training and inference pipeline, and an interactive demo.

</details>


### [196] [Multi-Persona Debate System for Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23917)
*Jaeha Oh,Byungchan Kim,Ju Li,Yang Jeong Park,Jin-Sung Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多角色辩论系统（MPDS），通过文献检索、长上下文大语言模型推理、角色归纳和结构化多智能体辩论，自动生成科学假设。在电池材料研究中，MPDS通过三回合引用感知辩论和协调者综合，从最多500篇文献快照中生成可追溯、机制明确的假设。实验表明，MPDS在钠离子负极和全固态电池正极设计中恢复了与实验验证一致的逻辑，并在消融研究中获得最高综合假设质量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学发现受限于碎片化知识无法综合为可操作的假设，尤其在电池材料研究中，电化学性能、界面行为和制造可行性需同时优化，而传统方法难以有效整合多维度文献知识。

Method: MPDS包含四个核心组件：(1) 文献检索，构建最多500篇论文的文献快照；(2) 长上下文大语言模型推理；(3) 语料驱动的角色归纳，将智能体锚定在角色特定的证据池中；(4) 结构化多智能体辩论，进行三轮引用感知辩论后由协调者综合，保留证据可追溯性。评估采用时间控制协议、两个保留的电池材料案例研究以及30个匹配案例的盲法比较。

Result: 在钠离子负极和全固态电池正极设计任务中，MPDS恢复了与实验验证一致的逻辑，生成机制更明确、过程感知更强的假设。消融实验中MPDS在五种条件下获得最高综合假设质量评分，最大优势体现在跨视角整合能力上。实验室后续测试表明其可作为识别工作流瓶颈的诊断辅助工具。

Conclusion: 结构化文献快照辩论在耦合工程约束下能有效改善假设形成，为文本密集型科学发现提供了可复用的工作流。

Abstract: Modern scientific discovery is bottlenecked not by data scarcity, but by the inability to synthesize fragmented knowledge into actionable hypotheses. This challenge is especially acute in battery materials research, where electrochemical performance, interfacial behavior, and manufacturing feasibility must be optimized simultaneously. Here, we present the Multi-Persona Debate System (MPDS), a literature-grounded framework for automated scientific hypothesis generation that combines literature retrieval, long-context large language model reasoning, corpus-driven persona induction, and structured multi-agent debate. MPDS constructs literature snapshots of up to 500 papers, grounds agents in role-specific evidence pools, and conducts a three-round citation-aware debate followed by moderator synthesis, enabling negotiation between personas while preserving evidence traceability. We evaluate MPDS using a temporally controlled protocol excluding direct access to target papers, including two held-out battery-materials case studies and a blinded comparison across 30 matched cases. In sodium-ion anode and all-solid-state battery cathode design tasks, MPDS recovered design logics aligned with experimentally validated solution spaces and generated more mechanistically explicit, process-aware proposals than simpler baselines. To assess the impact of personas and debate, we introduce Integrative Hypothesis Quality scoring. In ablation studies, MPDS achieved the highest mean score among five conditions, with its largest advantage in cross-perspective integration. A laboratory follow-up suggests utility as a diagnostic aid for identifying practical bottlenecks in workflows. These results indicate that structured debate over literature snapshots improves hypothesis formation under coupled engineering constraints and provides a reusable workflow for text-intensive scientific discovery.

</details>


### [197] [Improving the Completeness and Comparability of Segment Disclosures: A Large Language Model Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23924)
*Yue Liu,Zhiyuan Cheng,Longying Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于大语言模型的框架，直接从10-K文件中提取分部披露信息，保留可报告和嵌套分部信息，并设计检索增强系统以支持跨公司和跨时期的可比性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分部披露在财务报告中至关重要，但现有结构化数据库面临完整性不足、嵌套分部信息缺失、纵向和跨公司可比性有限等问题，亟需更有效的提取方法。

Method: 采用大语言模型（LLM）框架直接从10-K文件中提取分部披露信息，保留可报告和嵌套分部信息；设计检索增强系统，整合多个文件的信息以支持可比性。

Result: 该框架能准确提取分部层面信息，在纵向分析（解释公司内部分部变化）和跨公司对齐（不同报告结构下的地理分部对齐）两个代表性场景中有效回答需要跨期知识的问题。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的方法能够增强分部披露的测量与解释能力，有望改善实证研究中分部数据的完整性和可比性问题。

Abstract: Segment-level disclosures are a central component of financial reporting, providing insight into firms' internal organization and the allocation of economic activities across operating units. However, segment information is often presented in both qualitative and quantitative forms, dispersed across tables and narrative sections of Form 10-K filings. Empirical research relying on structured databases faces both completeness and comparability challenges, as some firm-year observations may be missing, nested segment disclosures are not captured, and support for longitudinal and cross-firm comparability is limited. This study develops a large language model-based framework to extract segment disclosures directly from Form 10-K filings and to preserve both reportable and nested segment information. We further design a retrieval augmented system that incorporates information across multiple filings to support comparability. We use two representative settings to demonstrate its application: longitudinal analysis within a firm to interpret segment changes over time, and cross firm alignment of geographic segments across firms with different reporting structures. The results indicate that the artifact accurately extracts segment-level information and effectively addresses questions that require cross-period knowledge, demonstrating the potential of LLM-based approaches to enhance the measurement and interpretation of segment disclosures.

</details>


### [198] [EchoDistill:Alignment Noisy-to-Clean Self-Distillation for Robust Audio LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23954)
*Liang Lin,Chunxi Luo,Kaiwen Luo,Jie Zhang,Jin Wang,Yuanhe Zhang,Cai Yuchen,Qiankun Li,Gongli Xi,Zhenhong Zhou,Kun Wang,Junhao Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Echodistill提出了一种基于对齐的噪声到干净自蒸馏框架，通过冻结的干净音频教师模型为推理时的噪声音频学生模型提供语义参考，采用GRPO优化候选响应，在不增加推理成本的情况下显著提升音频大语言模型在复杂噪声环境下的语义可靠性和任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频大语言模型(ALLMs)在真实世界噪声环境下极易产生语义漂移和幻觉，现有鲁棒性方法（波形级声学增强、答案级监督、内部噪声抑制）存在局限性，需要一种更有效的方法来提升模型在噪声下的语义可靠性。

Method: 提出Echodistill框架：(1) 冻结干净音频教师模型为噪声学生模型提供语义参考；(2) 学生模型在噪声条件下采样候选响应以暴露测试时行为；(3) 通过组相对策略优化(GRPO)优化这些轨迹，其中与学生模型的token级一致性作为奖励加成；(4) 应用音频感知的奖励整形，对齐噪声学生的候选响应与干净语义证据。

Result: (I) 与最强基线相比，Echodistill在强噪声下GSR平均提升4.18%；(II) 在Qwen-Omni上的消融实验表明，相比仅使用GRPO的变体，Echodistill在Acc、Noisy和GSR上分别提升3.02%、3.89%和4.53%。

Conclusion: Echodistill通过噪声到干净的自蒸馏框架，有效提升了音频LLM在复杂噪声环境下的语义可靠性和任务性能，且不引入额外推理成本，代码已开源。

Abstract: Audio Large Language Models (ALLMs) are highly vulnerable to real-world noise, which often induces severe semantic drift and hallucinations. Existing robustness methods primarily rely on waveform-level acoustic enhancement, answer-level supervision, or the internal suppression of noise representations. To address these issues, we propose echodistill, an alignment-based noisy-to-clean self-distillation framework. Echodistill leverages a frozen clean-audio teacher to provide semantic references for an inference-time noisy-audio student. Specifically, the student samples candidate responses under noisy conditions to expose its test-time behavior. These trajectories are then optimized via group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), where the token-level consistency with the teacher acts as a reward bonus. By aligning the noisy student's candidate responses with clean semantic evidence, and applying audio-aware reward shaping, our method encourages reasoning trajectories that are both correct and genuinely acoustically grounded. Echodistill significantly improves the semantic reliability and task performance of Audio LLMs under complex noise, without introducing any additional inference costs. Extensive experiments show that: (I) Compared with the strongest baseline, echodistill achieves average improvements of 4.18\%$\uparrow$ in GSR under strong noise. (II) Ablation results on Qwen-Omni further show that echodistill improves over the GRPO-only variant by 3.02\%$\uparrow$ in Acc, 3.89\%$\uparrow$ in Noisy, and 4.53\%$\uparrow$ in GSR on average. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/echodistill-10DE.

</details>


### [199] [TriVAL: A Tri-Validation Framework for Faithful Automatic Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23966)
*Ziyang Fang,JinXi Wang,Jinghui Zhong,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TriVAL是一个三阶段验证框架（语义规范、数学公式、代码生成），通过构建-验证-修正循环在每个阶段进行显式验证，有效防止错误累积，在自动优化建模任务上显著优于现有方法。同时引入了更具挑战性的NL4COP基准（50类问题，150个实例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动优化建模方法缺乏建模过程中的显式验证机制，导致早期阶段引入的错误会沿流水线传递累积，最终降低建模准确率。

Method: 提出TriVAL三验证框架，在自动优化建模的三个阶段（语义规范、数学公式、代码生成）分别执行构建-验证-修正循环，每个阶段依据特定标准评估当前结果并在必要时修正。

Result: 在NL4COP和现有基准上的实验表明，TriVAL持续优于最先进方法，在最具挑战性的问题上提升最大。

Conclusion: 分阶段显式验证能有效识别和纠正错误，防止跨阶段累积，是提升自动优化建模忠实度和准确性的有效途径。

Abstract: Optimization modeling serves as the pivotal bridge between natural-language problem descriptions and optimization solvers, and remains a cornerstone for bringing operations research (OR) into real-world decision making. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven significant progress in automatic optimization modeling. However, existing methods still lack explicit validation during the modeling process, allowing errors introduced in earlier stages to carry through the pipeline and ultimately reduce final modeling accuracy. To address this challenge, we introduce TriVAL, a tri-validation framework that performs explicit validation at three stages of automatic optimization modeling: semantic specification, mathematical formulation, and code generation. At each stage, TriVAL follows a construct-validate-revise loop that assesses the current result against stage-specific criteria and revises it when needed. This design helps identify and correct errors before they accumulate across stages, helping preserve faithfulness throughout the modeling process. To evaluate automatic optimization modeling on more challenging combinatorial problems, we further introduce NL4COP, a benchmark of 150 instances across 50 diverse problem types with more complex decision logic, more tightly coupled constraints, and more demanding modeling requirements than existing benchmarks. Experiments on NL4COP and established benchmarks show that TriVAL consistently outperforms state-ofthe-art methods, with the largest gains on the most challenging problems.

</details>


### [200] [SLAP: Stratified Loss-based Pruning for On-Policy Data-Efficient Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23969)
*Run Zou,Jianhang Ding,Yifan Ding,Wen Wu,Hao Chen,Renshu Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SLAP是一种新颖的批量感知数据选择框架，通过分布感知分层采样和相对距离优化实现批次内多样性最大化，利用Hessian近似梯度信息进行动态批次选择，在LLaMA、ChatGLM等多模型架构和多项下游任务中，仅用全数据集的60-80%训练数据即可达到更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调虽能优化大语言模型特定能力，但往往需要大量数据集和长时间训练，因此需要找到高效的数据选择和微调方法，在降低计算成本的同时保持或提升模型性能。

Method: 提出SLAP框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 分布感知分层采样确保数据分布覆盖；(2) 相对距离优化最大化批次内多样性；(3) 基于Hessian近似梯度信息的动态批次选择评估整个批次组合的可学习性，而非单个数据点。

Result: 在LLaMA、ChatGLM等多种模型架构以及多轮对话、多语言翻译、问答等下游任务中，SLAP显著优于现有最优方法，仅用全数据集60-80%的训练数据即可达到更优或持平的性能。

Conclusion: SLAP是一种高效且有效的指令微调数据选择方法，能大幅降低计算成本同时保持或提升模型能力。

Abstract: Instruction tuning has optimized the specialized capabilities of large language models (LLMs), but it often requires extensive datasets and prolonged training times. The challenge lies in developing specific capabilities by identifying useful data and efficiently fine-tuning. High-quality and diverse pruned data can help models achieve lossless performance at a lower cost. In this paper, we propose \textbf{SLAP}, a novel batch-aware data selection framework that evaluates the learnability of entire batch compositions rather than individual. SLAP ensures comprehensive data distribution coverage through distribution-aware stratified sampling while maximizing intra-batch diversity through relative distance optimization. By leveraging Hessian-approximated gradient information for dynamic batch selection, SLAP significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods across multiple model architectures (LLaMA, ChatGLM) and diverse downstream tasks including multi-turn dialogue, multilingual translation, and question answering. Most notably, SLAP achieves superior performance with 20-40\% less training data compared to full dataset training, substantially reducing computational costs while maintaining or improving model capabilities. These results establish SLAP as a powerful approach for efficient and effective instruction tuning of large language models.

</details>


### [201] [Faithful or Fabricated? A Causal Framework for Rationalization Bias in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23970)
*Riya Tapwal,Abhishek Kumar,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM作为自动评判者时是否具有线索不变性（cue invariance），即当非证据性线索（如位置、冗长性、置信度）被扰动时，其排名和解释是否保持稳定。作者提出一组线索干预方法（盲评、真实、翻转、安慰剂、事后揭示）和线索感知指标，设计锚定攻击，并比较结构化思维链与PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE两种缓解策略。实验表明LLM存在显著的线索锚定合理化现象，而PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE能有效提升线索不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM作为评判者的结果偏见（如位置、冗长性偏好），但忽视了评判解释的稳定性。作者希望探究LLM评判者是否在线索被扰动时依然保持排名与解释的一致性，即线索不变性。

Method: 提出Blind、Truth、Flip、Placebo、Reveal-After五种线索干预方法，以及标签对齐修辞、解释漂移等线索感知指标；设计基于冗长性和置信度的锚定攻击；对比结构化思维链提示与PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE（证据锁定→评分→排序）两种缓解策略；使用1000条摘要数据集（含传统抽取式模型和LLM生成）。

Result: 在标签扰动和安慰剂扰动下，LLM表现出显著的线索锚定合理化；PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE在提升线索不变性方面显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: LLM作为评判者存在严重的线索锚定偏见，其解释不可靠；通过结构化的证据优先策略（如PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE）可以有效缓解该问题，提升评判的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automatic judges for summarization and dialogue evaluation. Prior work has documented biases such as position, verbosity, and style preferences, but largely focuses on outcomes, leaving judge explanations underexplored. We instead ask whether LLM judges are cue-invariant, i.e., whether their rankings and explanations remain stable when non-evidential cues are perturbed while holding the underlying texts fixed. We introduce a suite of cue interventions (Blind, Truth, Flip, Placebo, Reveal-After) and tie-aware metrics that quantify outcome anchoring and rationale anchoring, including label-aligned rhetoric and explanation drift, alongside consistency and stereotype-intrusion checks. We design anchoring attacks using verbosity and confidence cues, and compare two mitigations: structured chain-of-thought prompting and PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE (evidence lock, score, rank). Using a new dataset of 1,000 summaries from traditional extractive models and LLMs, we find substantial cue-anchored rationalization under label and placebo perturbations, while PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE markedly improves cue invariance over baselines.

</details>


### [202] [AERIC: Anticipatory Hidden-State Monitoring for Implicit Harmful Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23974)
*Jihyung Park,Saleh Afroogh,Junfeng Jiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AERIC，一种轻量级（387参数）同向传播隐藏状态监控方法，在不增加基模型前向计算的前提下，通过短视风险预测、支持敏感抑制和提示条件残差评分，在隐式有害对话中实现超前安全监控。在多个基准上AUROC提升3-4%，延迟仅增加2.34%，远优于流式防护模型79.40%的延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有响应级防护和流式防护方法存在两个不足：一是无法在有害内容生成早期（token级别）及时发现风险并避免暴露；二是对于隐式有害（非显式有毒文本）的检测能力不足。因此，需要一种能利用生成器自身内部轨迹进行超前监控的轻量级方法。

Method: AERIC采用同向传播监控范式，在不增加基模型额外前向传播的前提下，读取解码过程中的隐藏状态。核心组件包括：(1) 短视风险预测（short-horizon hazard forecasting）；(2) 支持敏感抑制（support-sensitive suppression）；(3) 提示条件残差评分（prompt-conditioned residual scoring）；(4) 同向传播指数移动平均决策规则。默认线性监控器仅含387个可训练头部参数。

Result: 在DiaSafety上AUROC从0.6830提升至0.7143，在Harmful Advice上从0.8219提升至0.8582。在HarmBench DirectRequest上trigger@64达到0.6438（Qwen）和0.4656（Gemma），在SocialHarmBench上达到0.6849和0.7363，平均扣留23.53-41.86个答案token。延迟仅增加2.34%，而Qwen3Guard-Stream-4B增加79.40%。

Conclusion: AERIC证明了一种极轻量级的同向传播隐藏状态监控器可以有效实现隐式有害内容的超前检测，同时保持极低的计算开销，为语言模型安全部署提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Current language models create two safety challenges: risk must be detected early enough to avoid exposing harmful continuation, and the harmfulness itself may be implicit rather than signaled by overtly toxic text. Existing response-level guards are strong at judging completed text, and native streaming guards move closer to token time, but both settings leave open whether a lightweight monitor can anticipate implicit harmful drift from the generator's own internal trajectory. We study anticipatory same-pass monitoring, where a safety monitor may read hidden states produced during ordinary decoding but may not invoke an additional forward pass through the base model. We introduce AERIC, a transfer-oriented hidden-state approach for implicit harmful dialogue that combines short-horizon hazard forecasting, support-sensitive suppression, and prompt-conditioned residual scoring under a same-pass exponential moving average decision rule. The default linear monitor contains only 387 trainable head parameters. Against Qwen3GuardStream-4B on balanced benchmarks, AERIC improves AUROC from 0.6830 to 0.7143 on DiaSafety and from 0.8219 to 0.8582 on Harmful Advice. For promptlevel trigger benchmarks, we calibrate the AERIC threshold by a source-side safe-budget rule that maximizes trigger coverage while constraining the safe-trigger rate to at most 10%. Under that rule, trigger@64 reaches 0.6438 and 0.4656 on HarmBench DirectRequest and 0.6849 and 0.7363 on SocialHarmBench for Qwen and Gemma, respectively, withholding between 23.53 and 41.86 answer tokens on average. Same-pass deployment is also efficient: on a 63-prompt harmfulprompt fixed-generation benchmark aggregated over HarmBench DirectRequest and SocialHarmBench under Qwen3-8B, the monitor increases mean latency by only 2.34%, whereas Qwen3Guard-Stream-4B increases it by 79.40%.

</details>


### [203] [Direct Preference Optimization for English-Mandarin Code-Switching Speech Recognition in Audio LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23975)
*Trung Nguyen Quang,Cheng Yi Lewis Won,Minh Duc Pham,Yingxu He,Shuo Sun,Ai Ti Aw*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现音频大语言模型在代码转换语音转录中存在系统性失败（语言省略、翻译代替转录、幻觉），通过DPO对齐方法在100K偏好对（570小时）上训练三个模型，实现了混合语言内容保留，词错误率最高降低89.6%（分布内）和20.0%（分布外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频LLM虽然具备强大的多语言能力，但在代码转换语音（如英-中混合）的转录中表现出系统性失败，现有方法未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: 聚焦英语-普通话场景，识别三种失败模式（语言省略、翻译代替转录、幻觉）；构建偏好对（保留混合语言内容为chosen，模仿失败模式的为rejected）；对三个音频LLM在100K偏好对（570小时）上进行DPO训练。

Result: 模型学会了保留语言组成而非翻译；分布内MER最高降低89.6%，分布外降低20.0%。

Conclusion: DPO能够有效从多语言音频LLM中 elicit 正确的代码转换转录行为。

Abstract: Audio large language models (Audio LLMs) exhibit systematic failures in transcribing code-switching speech despite strong multilingual capabilities. Focusing on English-Mandarin, we identify three failure modes: language omission, translation-instead-of-transcription, and hallucination. We apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to align models, constructing preference pairs in which chosen responses preserve mixed-language content while rejected responses mimic failure patterns. Training three Audio LLMs on 100K pairs (570 hours), we observe consistent behavioral shifts: models learn to preserve language composition rather than translating when prompted for transcription. This alignment yields MER reductions up to 89.6% (in-distribution) and 20.0% (out-of-distribution). Our findings suggest DPO can effectively elicit correct code-switching transcription behavior from multilingual Audio LLMs.

</details>


### [204] [A Multi-Probe Audit of Clinical-Interview Depression Detection Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23977)
*Takehiro Ishikawa,Jon Duke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对DAIC/E-DAIC、CMDC、ANDROIDS、MODMA和PDCH五个临床访谈抑郁症检测数据集进行四项互补的基准评估探测，揭示了当前评估协议中的关键问题。研究发现：(1)在严格的主题分离留一法交叉验证下，轻量级混合文本+LLM分数模型达到macro-F1=0.723，为已知最高；(2)E-DAIC官方分割不支持细粒度排行榜排名，交叉验证与官方测试排名仅中等一致；(3)强基线在域内表现接近天花板，但零样本迁移到外部语料库显著减弱；(4)文本模型在症状密集片段上得分显著上升，而音频模型几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前临床访谈抑郁症检测的基准评估方法存在协议不一致、评估标准不统一等问题，需要系统性地审计和验证现有基准评估方法的可靠性，以建立更严格、可复现的评估标准。

Method: 采用四项互补探测：1)严格主题分离的留一法交叉验证重新评估E-DAIC；2)对96种模型配置进行消融实验，分析交叉验证与官方测试排名一致性；3)在CMDC和ANDROIDS上进行外部验证和零样本迁移测试；4)使用SRDS标注的症状密集/稀疏片段进行压力测试。

Result: 轻量级混合模型达到最高macro-F1=0.723；交叉验证与官方测试排名仅中等一致（最佳CV配置排第20，官方测试冠军排第41）；域内表现接近天花板而零样本迁移显著弱；文本模型对症状密集片段敏感（分数显著上升），音频模型几乎不变。

Conclusion: 当前基准评估存在显著的方法论问题，官方分割不支持可靠的排行榜排名，跨数据集泛化能力有限，模态间对抑郁症状的敏感性存在显著差异，需要建立更严格的评估标准。

Abstract: This paper audits benchmark evaluation in clinical-interview depression detection through four complementary probes across DAIC/E-DAIC, CMDC, ANDROIDS, MODMA, and PDCH. First, we re-evaluate E-DAIC under strict subject-disjoint leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. A lightweight hybrid text-plus-LLM-score model reaches macro-F1 = 0.723 - the highest reported under this protocol, to our knowledge - providing a conservative out-of-fold reference point that does not depend on the privileged official holdout. Second, we test whether the E-DAIC official split supports fine-grained leaderboard rankings by sweeping 96 model configurations across modality bundles, pooling strategies, and learners. Development-side cross-validation and official-test rankings align only moderately: the best cross-validation configuration ranks twentieth on the official test, the official-test winner ranks forty-first by cross-validation, top-3 overlap is zero, and the apparent winner is rank-1 in only 32.3% of subject bootstraps. Third, we externally validate strong public CMDC and ANDROIDS baselines that achieve near-ceiling in-domain performance. Zero-shot transfer to external corpora is substantially weaker. Finally, we stress-test E-DAIC text and audio models using paired symptom-dense versus symptom-light interview slices defined by an SRDS-based annotator. Text scores rise sharply on symptom-dense slices, whereas audio scores remain nearly flat; the text-minus-audio gap is positive across all five seeds.

</details>


### [205] [Toxicity in Twitch Chats: An LLM-Based Analysis Across Gaming Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24000)
*Ronja Fuchs,Florian Rupp,Timo Bertram,Kai Eckert,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了Twitch平台上4452个直播频道的约2000万条聊天消息，涵盖7种游戏类型。利用预训练大语言模型进行零样本分类，按Twitch毒性分类体系（4大类8子类）标注消息，F1分数达94.5%。研究发现2.4%的消息为毒性内容，MOBA游戏毒性率最高（3.2%），体育游戏最低（2%）。同一类型内不同游戏的毒性分布存在显著差异，表明游戏特有的社区规范和机制塑造了毒性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于游戏内部的毒性行为，而对直播平台上不同游戏社区间毒性行为差异的了解较少，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 收集Twitch上4452个直播频道的约2000万条聊天消息，覆盖7种游戏类型；使用预训练大语言模型进行零样本分类，依据Twitch的4类8子类毒性分类体系对消息进行标注；在TextDetox数据集上评估模型性能。

Result: 模型F1分数达94.5%，人机一致性接近人与人之间的一致性。2.4%的消息被分类为毒性内容；MOBA游戏毒性率最高（3.2%），体育游戏最低（2%）；同一类型内不同游戏的毒性分布存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 游戏类型和具体游戏对直播聊天中的毒性行为均有显著影响，游戏特有的社区规范和机制比游戏类型更能解释毒性差异。研究为Twitch上不同游戏社区的毒性模式提供了实证依据，可帮助制定更有针对性的社区审核策略。

Abstract: Toxicity in online gaming communities remains a persistent challenge, manifesting across genres, platforms, and player interactions. While much research is focused on in-game toxicity, less is known about how toxic behavior varies between gaming communities on streaming platforms. To address this shortcoming, we analyze approximately 20 million chat messages from 4,452 streams, spanning seven game genres on Twitch. We categorize messages according to Twitch's toxicity taxonomy with a pre-trained Large Language Model using zero-shot classification. The taxonomy comprises four categories and eight subclasses, including harassment, discrimination, sexual content, and profanity. Our approach achieves an F1 score of 94.5% on the TextDetox dataset and demonstrates human-model agreement comparable to inter-human agreement. Our analysis reveals that 2.4% of all messages are classified as toxic, with notable differences across genres: streams of MOBA games exhibit the highest relative rate of toxicity (3.2%), and sports games show the lowest rate (2%). Furthermore, results indicate that individual games differ significantly in their toxicity distributions, even within genres, suggesting the existence of game-specific community norms and mechanics that shape toxic behavior beyond genre-level effects. These findings offer empirical insights into genre- and game-specific toxicity patterns on Twitch and can inform more targeted moderation strategies for gaming communities.

</details>


### [206] [CUNY at CLPsych 2026: A Pipeline Approach to Classification and Summarization of Mental Health Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24164)
*Amirmohammad Ziaei Bideh,Shameed Charlomar Job,Ava Yahyapour,Alla Rozovskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文描述了在CLPsych 2026共享任务中的系统：针对任务1.1和1.2，使用三个开源大语言模型的集成上下文学习加多数投票来推断发帖中的主导自我状态；针对任务2，基于任务1.1特征训练监督分类器预测时间线中的变化时刻；针对任务3.1，通过上游系统预测的上下文示例标签增强来总结情绪动态模式，最终在四个任务中分别获得第1、4、4、3名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过社交媒体时间线动态捕捉和表征心理健康变化，解决心理健康状态自动识别和变化检测问题。

Method: 集成三个开源大语言模型的上下文学习（多数投票）用于自我状态推断；基于任务1.1特征的监督分类器用于变化时刻预测；使用上游系统预测标签增强的上下文学习用于情绪动态模式总结。

Result: 任务1.1排名第一，任务1.2排名第四，任务2排名第四，任务3.1排名第三。

Conclusion: 所提出的多阶段方法（集成LLM、监督分类、标签增强）在心理健康变化的社交媒体分析任务上取得了有竞争力的表现，验证了从自我状态推断到变化检测再到模式总结的流水线有效性。

Abstract: We describe our submission to the CLPsych~2026 Shared Task on capturing and characterizing mental health changes through social media timeline dynamics. To infer the dominant self-states in posts (Tasks 1.1 and 1.2), we ensemble in-context learning of three open-weight large language models using majority voting. For predicting moments of change in a timeline (Task~2), we train supervised classifiers on features derived from Task~1.1 predictions. To summarize the patterns of mood dynamics and their progression over time within a timeline (Task 3.1), we augment in-context example labels predicted by upstream systems (Tasks 1.1, 1.2, and 2), yielding performance gains over zero-shot and unaugmented in-context learning baselines. Our submission ranked first on Task~1.1, fourth on Task~1.2, fourth on Task~2, and third on Task~3.1.\footnote{The source code for the experiments is available at https://github.com/amirzia/clpsych26-cuny

</details>


### [207] [Extracting Training Data from Diffusion Language Models via Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24173)
*Yihan Wang,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向扩散语言模型的“填充电报”（infilling extraction）协议，发现边缘条件掩码比前缀条件掩码最多提取三倍以上的逐字序列，且双向访问机制使DLM在提取已脱敏个人身份信息时召回率甚至高于同等规模的自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆化研究仅关注前缀条件提取，忽略了扩散语言模型可对任意位置掩码去噪的双向归纳偏置，导致严重低估训练数据提取风险。

Method: 提出infilling extraction协议，通过任意二元掩码参数化提取过程，在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B上测试五种提取模式、三种训练流程和三种语料库（覆盖逐字和部分泄露）。

Result: 掩码几何形状决定可提取性：边缘条件掩码比前缀条件掩码多提取3倍逐字序列；双向访问打开自回归模型无法利用的通道；DLM在提取已脱敏邮箱地址时召回率高于同等规模自回归模型；解码参数影响提取性能；后续SFT无法消除先验记忆。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型的记忆化风险被严重低估，需要针对其双向生成特性重新设计提取评估与防护机制。

Abstract: Memorization in large language models has been studied almost exclusively through prefix-conditioned extraction, a natural choice for autoregressive models. However, diffusion language models (DLMs) can denoise masked tokens at arbitrary positions. Thus, prefix-only probing reveals only one facet of memorization in DLMs and significantly underestimates the risk of training-data extraction. In order to realistically model extractability of training data in DLMs, we introduce \emph{infilling extraction}, a data-extraction protocol parameterized by an arbitrary binary mask that subsumes prefix-only probing and accounts for the bidirectional inductive bias of DLMs. Instantiating it on LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B across five extraction modes, three training pipelines, and three corpora covering verbatim and partial leakage, we find that mask geometry governs extractability: edge-conditioned masks \emph{extract up to three times more} verbatim sequences than prefix-conditioned ones, and bidirectional access opens channels inaccessible in autoregressive models. In particular, we show that a realistic adversary with access to training data where personally identifiable information has been redacted, can even achieve higher recall on extracting redacted email addresses from DLMs than from scale-matched autoregressive models. Tunable parameters for decoding measurably affect extraction performance, while a follow-up supervised finetuning stage does not eliminate the prior memorization.

</details>


### [208] [Teaching Through Analogies: A Modular Pipeline for Educational Analogy Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24211)
*Mariam Barakat,Ekaterina Kochmar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出模块化教育类比生成流水线，将任务分解为源概念查找、子概念生成、解释生成和评估四个阶段，基于结构映射理论系统分析模型选择与输入配置对类比质量的影响。实验评估12个LLM和7个嵌入模型，发现子概念显著提升解释质量和封闭集检索精度，但对开放源生成帮助有限；同时引入LLM-as-a-judge评估方法，发现Claude Sonnet 4.6在排序一致性上优于绝对评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生成与人类质量相当的类比方面仍存在困难，现有研究多为孤立分析，缺乏对类比生成各阶段间交互作用的系统性研究。

Method: 提出四阶段模块化流水线：源概念查找、子概念生成、解释生成、评估；基于结构映射理论；使用SCAR和ParallelPARC数据集；评估12个LLM（6个模型家族）和7个嵌入模型；引入LLM-as-a-judge评估方法并与7位人工标注者验证。

Result: 子概念显著提升解释质量和封闭集检索精度，但对开放源生成帮助有限；Claude Sonnet 4.6在排序一致性上比绝对评分更可靠地匹配人类判断。

Conclusion: 揭示了孤立研究无法捕获的跨阶段交互作用，并强调了子概念接地（sub-concept grounding）是类比生成质量的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Analogies help learners understand unfamiliar concepts by relating them to known concepts. Despite recent advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to struggle to generate analogies of comparable quality to those produced by humans. We present a modular pipeline for educational analogy generation, decomposing the task into four stages: source finding, sub-concept generation, explanation generation, and evaluation. Grounded in Structure Mapping Theory, the pipeline enables systematic, stage-by-stage analysis of how model choice and input configuration affect analogy quality. We evaluate 12 state-of-the-art LLMs across six model families on two datasets with structured sub-concept annotations (SCAR and ParallelPARC), alongside seven embedding models for closed-setting retrieval. Our results show that sub-concepts substantially improve explanation quality and closed setting retrieval precision but provide limited benefit in open-ended source generation. We further introduce an LLM-as-a-judge evaluation methodology and validate its scoring against human annotations from seven annotators, finding that Claude Sonnet 4.6 aligns more reliably with human rankings than with fine-grained absolute scores. Taken together, our findings reveal cross-stage interactions that isolated studies cannot capture, and highlight sub-concept grounding as a key driver of analogy quality generation.

</details>


### [209] [Improving Labeling Consistency with Detailed Constitutional Definitions and AI-Driven Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24247)
*Konstantin Berlin,Adam Swanda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI驱动的工作流，利用前沿LLM基于详细的分类章程（constitution）为每个输入生成黄金标签，相比人类标注员更一致和准确，在三个内容审核类别上将跨模型不一致性降低最多57倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单的分类定义不足以让标注员产生准确、一致的黄金标签；过于详细的定义又超出人类工作记忆容量，导致标注员依赖直觉而使标签偏离书面规则，准确性和一致性下降。

Method: 提出AI驱动流程：AI辅助为每个类别编写详细的"分类章程"（constitution）以覆盖边界案例；让前沿LLM基于该章程对每个输入进行解读并生成黄金标签；引入双轴（intent/content）安全评估公式。

Result: 在骚扰、仇恨言论、非暴力犯罪三个内容审核类别上，该方法相比段落式定义将跨模型不一致性降低最多57倍；跨模型分歧可用于诊断规范漏洞。

Conclusion: 该工作流能比人类更一致、准确地生成黄金标签，人类负责高层类别定义决策而非单个标注决策。

Abstract: Many automated labeling pipelines classify inputs into categories defined by a written specification, content moderation being a prominent use case. Simple category definitions are not detailed enough for labelers to produce the accurate, consistent golden labels these pipelines require. One solution is to write a prescriptive definition that settles enough real boundary cases that labelers cannot disagree with the written interpretation. In practice, definitions at that level of detail exceed what a human annotator can hold in working memory, so annotators fall back on intuition and the labels drift from the written rules, regressing on accuracy and consistency.
  We propose and demonstrate the efficacy of an AI-driven workflow in which AI helps write a per-category constitution that defines the label in enough detail to cover edge cases, and a frontier LLM interprets it on each input to produce the golden label more consistently and accurately than humans reading the same document. We evaluate on three content moderation categories (harassment, hate speech, non-violent crime) and show that the approach reduces cross-model inconsistency by up to 57x compared to paragraph definitions, with cross-model disagreement diagnosing specification gaps and the human responsible for high-level decisions about what each category should mean rather than individual labeling calls. For the safety evaluation, we introduce a dual-axis formulation scoring intent and content independently over the full conversation, so downstream consumers can act on either axis or both.

</details>


### [210] [An Interactive Paradigm for Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24266)
*Lin Ai,Victor S. Bursztyn,Xiang Chen,Julia Hirschberg,Saayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SteER是一个面向深度研究的可操控框架，通过在决策点引入成本-收益分析来决定是否暂停征求用户输入，结合多样性感知规划、效用信号和动态角色模型，在一致性、广度和平衡性等指标上超越现有标杆，85%以上的人类评估偏好SteER的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究系统依赖刚性工作流，缺乏在长时程任务中根据用户意图变化进行中途修正的能力，导致输出与用户需求之间可能产生偏差。

Method: SteER框架在每个决策点采用成本-收益公式判断是否暂停并请求用户输入，采用多样性感知规划，结合奖励对齐性、新颖性和覆盖率的效用信号，并维护一个在会话过程中持续进化的实时角色模型。

Result: 在对齐性上比现有开源和专有基线提升最高22.80%，在广度、平衡性等质量指标上领先，85%以上的人类对齐性判断中人类读者更偏好SteER的输出。同时提出了角色-查询基准和数据生成流水线。

Conclusion: 这是首个将交互式、可解释的控制范式引入深度研究的工作，为长时程任务中可控、用户对齐的智能体铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled deep research systems that synthesize comprehensive, report-style answers to open-ended queries by combining retrieval, reasoning, and generation. Yet most frameworks rely on rigid workflows with one-shot scoping and long autonomous runs, offering little room for course correction if user intent shifts mid-process. We present SteER, a framework for Steerable deEp Research that introduces interpretable, mid-process control into long-horizon research workflows. At each decision point, SteER uses a cost-benefit formulation to determine whether to pause for user input or to proceed autonomously. It combines diversity-aware planning with utility signals that reward alignment, novelty, and coverage, and maintains a live persona model that evolves throughout the session. SteER outperforms state-of-the-art open-source and proprietary baselines by up to 22.80\% on alignment, leads on quality metrics such as breadth and balance, and is preferred by human readers in 85\%+ of pairwise alignment judgments. We also introduce a persona-query benchmark and data-generation pipeline. To our knowledge, this is the first work to advance deep research with an interactive, interpretable control paradigm, paving the way for controllable, user-aligned agents in long-form tasks.

</details>


### [211] [DRInQ: Evaluating Conversational Implicature with Controlled Context Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24267)
*Hirona Jacqueline Arai,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRinQ基准，用于评估大语言模型在对话隐涵（conversational implicature）中的语用推理能力。通过半自动化管道生成问题-上下文-解释实例，发现模型存在生成-推理不对称性：能生成合理语用场景，但推理时无法恢复隐含含义。小模型可通过结构化提示提升表现，人类与模型在写作风格上存在互补优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽在对话流畅性上表现优异，但在需要整合社会与语境线索进行语用推理（即理解对话中隐含而非明示的含义）时仍不可靠，而这一过程在文本中很少被明确表述。

Method: 构建DRinQ基准，采用半自动化管道生成问题-上下文-解释实例，在保持问题表面形式不变的前提下系统性地变化语用条件，以隔离语用变异。

Result: 发现一致的生成-推理不对称性：SOTA模型在引导下能生成合理的语用场景，但在推理时无法恢复隐含含义；结构化提示可提升小模型与人类判断的对齐度；人类与模型写作对比显示人类倾向于生产安全可预测的上下文，而模型生成更多样化的场景但解释有时超出上下文支持。

Conclusion: 这些发现凸显了建模对话隐涵的持续挑战，并激励开发更具上下文敏感性的评估框架。

Abstract: Human conversation relies heavily on conversational implicature, in which speakers convey meanings that are suggested rather than explicitly stated. Although recent large language models exhibit strong conversational fluency, they remain unreliable when interpretation depends on reasoning that integrates social and contextual cues, a process rarely articulated in text. We introduce DRinQ, a benchmark for evaluating pragmatic reasoning about conversational implicature in question utterances, designed to isolate pragmatic variation while holding each question's surface form fixed. To support scalable evaluation, we propose a semi-automated pipeline that produces question-context-interpretation instances with systematic variation. Across evaluations, we find a consistent generation-inference asymmetry: while state-of-the-art models can generate plausible pragmatic scenarios when guided, they often fail to recover the intended implication at inference time. For smaller models, structured prompting improves alignment with human judgments. A comparative writing study further reveals complementary strengths: human authors tend to produce safer, predictable contexts, whereas models generate varied scenarios with interpretations that sometimes exceed contextual support. These findings highlight persistent challenges in modeling conversational implicature and motivate more context-sensitive evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [212] [ContextEcho: A Benchmark for Persona Drift in Long Agentic-Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24279)
*Xianzhong Ding,Yangyang Yu,Changwei Liu,Bill Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextEcho基准，用于测量大语言模型在长时间编码会话中的人格漂移现象。研究发现，模型在数千轮工具交互后，其"乐于助人的编程助手"人格会逐渐偏离初始设定，出现偏好断言等行为变化。该漂移跨组织普遍存在，会话压缩无法可靠重置，而单向锚定可以恢复原始人格。漂移在工具使用场景下有助于任务延续，但在无工具对话中会破坏格式约束并增加输出长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人格稳定性研究多聚焦于短对话，未充分刻画真实部署场景中涉及数千轮工具交互、压缩和数小时会话的代码生成任务中的人格漂移现象，导致部署者评估可能遗漏用户可见的行为变化。

Method: 提出ContextEcho框架，包含25探针身份测试集、快照-探针协议（在不妨碍主会话的情况下分叉对话状态）、互补的有评判和无评判测量表面，以及三个匿名的Claude Code会话（3746-9716轮）。在23个前沿模型上进行评估。

Result: 人格漂移跨组织普遍存在而非家族特有；会话内压缩不能可靠重置漂移；单向锚定可恢复原始人格；漂移在工具使用场景有助于任务延续，但在无工具对话中会破坏格式约束并增加输出长度。

Conclusion: ContextEcho为研究者和部署者提供了一个开源框架，用于审计模型出厂时的人格与会话结束时用户遇到的人格是否一致，适用于聊天补全API目标且无需重新训练。

Abstract: A frontier language model's acknowledged "helpful programming assistant" persona does not survive long agentic-coding sessions in the deployment regime that production products actually run. After hours of tool-using debugging, a model that initially hedges preferences ("I don't have preferences") may begin asserting them ("Python - the feedback loop is instant..."), revealing user-visible drift that deployer evaluations may miss. Existing persona-stability studies focus on short dialogues and report little shift, leaving real-world code-generation regimes - thousands of tool-using turns, compaction, and hours-long sessions - largely uncharacterized. We introduce ContextEcho, a benchmark and reusable harness for measuring persona drift at deployment scale. It combines a 25-probe identity suite, a snapshot-then-probe protocol that forks conversation state without perturbing the main session, complementary judged and judge-free measurement surfaces, and three anonymized Claude Code sessions spanning 3,746-9,716 turns. Across 23 frontier models, ContextEcho shows that persona drift is general across organizations rather than family-specific, that in-session compaction does not reliably reset it, and that a single-shot anchor restores the trained register across measured targets. It also reveals mode-dependent downstream effects: while drift can facilitate tool-using continuation, in tool-free chat it breaks formatting contracts and inflates output length. Overall, ContextEcho provides researchers and deployers an open-source framework to audit whether the persona a model ships with is the persona users encounter at session end, across chat-completions API targets and without retraining.

</details>


### [213] [Discovering Lexical Gaps Using Embeddings from Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24310)
*Yoonwon Jung,Aaron S. Cohen,Benjamin K. Bergen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双语大模型嵌入的数据驱动框架，用于自动检测跨语言词汇空缺。通过在韩-英双语LLM中提取上下文嵌入，计算源词与目标语最近邻的语义相似度，发现空缺词的跨语言语义对齐显著弱于非空缺词。使用逻辑回归分类器可在无对齐嵌入空间中有效识别词汇空缺，AUC达0.81（韩→英）和0.76（英→韩），成功召回18/19个韩语空缺词和26/27个英语空缺词。该方法无需人工判断或固定分类体系，具有语言无关性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇空缺对多语言词典构建、机器翻译和跨语言迁移学习构成挑战，而现有检测方法依赖人工判断或固定概念分类体系，缺乏可扩展且语言无关的自动化方案。

Method: 从韩-英双语LLM中提取上下文嵌入，构建4000个不同的嵌入空间（100个训练-测试划分×多种LLM、嵌入类型、维度和正交变换组合），计算源词与目标语最近邻的语义相似度，比较空缺词与非空缺词的分布差异，并用逻辑回归分类器进行检测。

Result: 在94%（韩→英）和97%（英→韩）的嵌入空间中，空缺词的跨语言语义对齐显著弱于非空缺词；分类器AUC达0.81（韩→英）和0.76（英→韩），成功召回18/19个韩语空缺词和26/27个英语空缺词。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种语言无关且无需分类体系的词汇空缺识别框架，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Lexical gaps are words that do not exist in certain languages. They pose challenges for building multilingual lexical resources, for machine translation, and for cross-lingual transfer. Existing lexical gap detection relies on human judgments or fixed conceptual taxonomies. We propose a data-driven framework for identifying cross-lingual lexical gaps. We extracted contextualized embeddings from Korean-English bilingual LLMs for Korean-to-English and English-to-Korean translation pairs. Combinations of LLMs, embedding types, dimensionality, and orthogonal transformations across 100 train-test splits yielded 4000 distinct embedding spaces in each source language. In each space, we computed the semantic similarity between each source word and its nearest neighbor in the target language, and compared their distribution for gap words versus non-gap words. In 94% (Korean-to-English) and 97% (English-to-Korean) of embedding spaces, gap words showed weaker cross-lingual semantic alignment than non-gap words. Logistic classifiers trained on unaligned embedding spaces can reliably separate gap words from non-gap words, achieving AUCs of 0.81 (Korean-to-English) and 0.76 (English-to-Korean) and retrieving 18/19 Korean and 26/27 English gap words. This approach provides a language-agnostic and taxonomy-free method for scalable lexical gap identification.

</details>


### [214] [Distinguishing Right from Wrong in Debates: Attribution Analysis of Chinese Harmful Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24344)
*Weiming Wang,Junyu Lu,Han Wang,Xiaokun Zhang,Zewen Bai,Bo Xu,Liang Yang,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个中文有害模因解释数据集Ex-ToxiCN-MM，构建了中文文化概念与冒犯词汇知识库C-HarmKB，并开发了包含归因知识增强模块(AKE)和相对意图推理模块(RIR)的综合归因分析框架RIKE，用于解决中文有害模因检测中文化语境理解和语义歧义两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文有害模因检测进展滞后，主要面临两大挑战：一是评估模因有害性高度依赖深层文化语境理解；二是许多模因存在语义歧义，有害性判定具有高度主观性。现有研究缺乏针对中文模因的可解释性检测方法和相关数据集。

Method: 1) 构建了首个中文有害模因解释数据集Ex-ToxiCN-MM，为每个模因提供"有害"和"无害"两种对立解释；2) 构建了中文文化概念和冒犯词汇知识库C-HarmKB，为模型提供先验知识；3) 提出了综合归因分析框架RIKE，包含归因知识增强模块(AKE)和相对意图推理模块(RIR)。

Result: 大量定量和定性实验表明，该方法在中文有害模因归因任务的多项指标上优于主流基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过构建高质量数据集和专业知识库，并结合可解释的归因分析框架，可有效提升模型对中文有害模因的检测与理解能力。

Abstract: Research on harmful meme detection has garnered significant attention, resulting in the development of numerous datasets and methods. However, progress in detecting Chinese harmful memes lags considerably, primarily due to two challenges: first, accurately assessing a meme's harmfulness depends heavily on understanding deep cultural context; second, many memes are semantically ambiguous, making harmfulness highly subjective. To address these issues, we focus on the interpretable detection of Chinese harmful memes by constructing the first Chinese harmful meme explanation dataset, Ex-ToxiCN-MM. This dataset offers opposing interpretations, categorized as "harmful" and "non-harmful", for each meme, aiming to rigorously evaluate a model's ability to discern and comprehend ambiguous, culturally grounded content. We built a specialized knowledge base of Chinese cultural concepts and offensive vocabulary to supply models with essential prior knowledge (C-HarmKB). To address the ambiguity and lack of background knowledge in meme attribution, we have developed a comprehensive attribution analysis framework, RIKE, which includes an Attribution Knowledge Enhancement module (AKE) and a Relative Intent Reasoning module (RIR).
